# Skills · R · part 83

900 entries, remember to remix. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **remember**: Only inside an active Claudia session (the claudia skill already loaded) — update Claudia's working memory for this person — the distilled person model, goals, and safety flags under ~/.claudia/. Use when something worth carrying forward emerges in the conversation, and to run the one-time first-run disclosure that memory is saved locally. ([abernier/claudia](https://github.com/abernier/claudia/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/remember) · ★3)
- **remember**: Ingest records into the shared eidetic memory store so they can be recalled later. Drives `eidetic remember`: accepts one record as a JSON object, or a batch as NDJSON on stdin for bulk ingest. Upsert is idempotent by id (and dedups by content hash) — re-remembering updates in place, never duplicates. Stamps a `created` date on every record at ingest time. Accepts `supersedes` (id of the record this one replaces, for within-scope shadowing via `sweep`) and `links` (list of related-memory ids). The store lives at ~/.eidetic/memory (a home-dir path outside any git worktree), and the wrapper def… ([agentculture/culture-nodes](https://github.com/agentculture/culture-nodes/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/remember) · ★3)

- **remember**: Use whenever the user shares (or you discover) something that should survive session boundaries — architectural decisions, recurring user preferences, non-obvious constraints, "always do it this way" rules. Writes a tier-2 topic file in `.claude/memory/topics/`, adds a tier-1 pointer to the index, and snapshots any prior version to the lineage ledger. ([ArtMin96/forge-studio](https://github.com/ArtMin96/forge-studio/tree/HEAD/plugins/memory/skills/remember) · ★3)

- **remember**: Save, correct, or explicitly reorganize durable developer-local facts and preferences in workspace-memory or global user-memory MCP topic entities when the user explicitly invokes /remember. Ask which store to use unless the request explicitly selects a destination. Accept the following text as the update. ([brad-jones/brads-forge](https://github.com/brad-jones/brads-forge/tree/HEAD/.apm/skills/remember) · ★3)
- **remember**: Commit conversation decisions, knowledge, open threads, and observations to Cortex memory. ([BrightWayAI/cortex](https://github.com/BrightWayAI/cortex/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/remember) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **remember**: Save gate for persistent memories — confirm tier (long/short) and expiry with the user before writing, so hallucinated or transient facts never enter long-term memory. Use whenever saving a memory file, and when the expiry sweep reports archived memories. ([choiyounggi/groundwork](https://github.com/choiyounggi/groundwork/tree/HEAD/plugins/memory-loop/skills/remember) · ★3)
- **remember**: This skill should be used when the user asks to "remember this", "save this to my knowledge base", "record this insight", "把这个记到知识库", "记住这个", "存进知识库", or when a session produced a durable insight, decision, pitfall, or fact worth keeping for future AI sessions. ([donald-ada/anthrosidian](https://github.com/donald-ada/anthrosidian/tree/HEAD/skills/remember) · ★3)
- **remember**: Save something to memory for future sessions. Use when the user says "remember this", "save for later", "don't forget", or wants to persist any preference, fact, lesson, or note across sessions. ([edimuj/claude-mneme](https://github.com/edimuj/claude-mneme/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/remember) · ★3)
- **remember**: Store a durable fact in the user's long-term memory — a decision, a preference, a convention, a fact about a person or project — when the user states one or asks you to remember something. Never for secrets, credentials, small talk, or one-off requests. ([flytomoon/al-buddy-memory](https://github.com/flytomoon/al-buddy-memory/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/remember) · ★3)
- **remember**: Save a tight cross-session handoff note to .remember/remember.md so the next session — same agent or different — picks up cleanly. Use at the end of a working session, before context compaction, or whenever the user says to remember state for next time. ([G-0-B/FLOSS](https://github.com/G-0-B/FLOSS/tree/HEAD/skill-corpus/remember) · ★3)
- **remember**: Save information to persistent memory for cross-session recall. Stores preferences, conventions, decisions, and context. ([HaoNgo232/agent-bridge-kit](https://github.com/HaoNgo232/agent-bridge-kit/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/remember) · ★3 · 3 copies)
- **remember**: Capture durable OpenCode memories safely; use when the user invokes /remember or asks to remember a preference, decision, or reusable workflow fact. ([jackoske/dotfiles](https://github.com/jackoske/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/.config/opencode/skills/remember) · ★3)
- **remember**: Save feature context for future sessions. Use when finishing significant work, after important discussions, or when the user says "save context". Captures decisions, corrections, architecture, and deliberately deferred work. ([koomai/claude-kit](https://github.com/koomai/claude-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/remember) · ★3)
- **remember**: Save an explicit fact or decision into the user's memory ([lavox-app/lavox](https://github.com/lavox-app/lavox/tree/HEAD/skills/remember) · ★3)
- **remember**: Save something to Mash memory on request. Use when the person runs /mash:remember, or says "remember this", "save this to Mash", or asks you to keep a decision, preference, correction or anything else for later sessions. ([mashhq/mash](https://github.com/mashhq/mash/tree/HEAD/plugins/mash/skills/remember) · ★3)
- **remember**: Recap surgery protocol — run when your context window approaches the wall (~80%+ of the 1M window), or when the selfrecap server's context nudge tells you to. Get your plan from the server, find a clean cut point in your own transcript, author an in-voice recap of the period being cut, dry-run it, execute with --go, then restart (yourself with `selfrecap restart` on Windows when configured, otherwise your operator restarts you). The AI-facing companion to the selfrecap server. ([nickshvab/selfrecap](https://github.com/nickshvab/selfrecap/tree/HEAD/skills/remember) · ★3)
- **remember**: Use when the user tells you something worth keeping (a fact, a decision, who owns or works on what, who said what) or explicitly says "remember ...". Store it in Mnemo's memory graph so it persists across sessions. ([oyekamal/mnemo-agent](https://github.com/oyekamal/mnemo-agent/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/remember) · ★3)
- **remember**: Save what matters at the end of a session so the next session picks up exactly where you left off. Or restore context at the start of a new session so nothing is lost between them — and never trust this conversation's own memory over what's actually on disk. ([partikularwaters/Banka](https://github.com/partikularwaters/Banka/tree/HEAD/skills-kit/remember) · ★3)

- **remember**: The one door into the owner's memory — take a fact the owner wants kept ("remember that…", "note this", "don't forget…"), route it by shape to the right file under my/ (a date that should warn, a topic to watch, a fact about them, or the general notes drawer), tag its source, confirm in plain words, commit. Use whenever the owner asks the kit to remember, note, or not forget something. ([crblabs/everyday-agents](https://github.com/crblabs/everyday-agents/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/remember) · ★2)

- **remember**: Persists reusable lessons and Architecture Briefs to the harness memory surfaces. ([Dfintz/Fintz-harness-kit](https://github.com/Dfintz/Fintz-harness-kit/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/remember) · ★2)

- **remember**: Reviews conversation history and captures valuable knowledge (best practices, coding conventions, architecture decisions, workflows, and user feedback) into persistent memory (AGENTS.md) or reusable skills. - **remember**: Record a durable decision, preference, convention, or correction into AGENTS.md so it applies in every future session. ([geminixiang/pi-stuff](https://github.com/geminixiang/pi-stuff/tree/HEAD/deprecated/pi-remember/skills/remember) · ★2)
- **remember**: Use when the friend wants to see what the system remembers about a project (or the wiki overall) — "what do you remember about X", "show me my project memory." Triggers on the /ren:remember [project] slash command. Renders the L2 map in human prose, not a raw markdown dump. The cheapest trust-builder in the scope (spec §3.8 A-10) — read-only, always. ([hazarsozer/ren-os](https://github.com/hazarsozer/ren-os/tree/HEAD/skills/remember) · ★2)
- **remember**: Continuous memory for Codex — write explicit remembers, and recall from Concord's own tiers when a question reaches past what session start injected. Not for saving state to resume a task mid-flight — that is session-handoff. Not for recording project decisions, rationale, or specs — even phrased as for-future-sessions, durable project knowledge belongs in the repo's spec tree (technical-spec), not session memory. - **remember**: Ingest records into the shared eidetic memory store so they can be recalled later. Drives `eidetic remember`: accepts one record as a JSON object, or a batch as NDJSON on stdin for bulk ingest. Upsert is idempotent by id (and dedups by content hash) — re-remembering updates in place, never duplicates. Stamps a `created` date on every record at ingest time. Accepts `supersedes` (id of the record this one replaces, for within-scope shadowing via `sweep`) and `links` (list of related-memory ids). Drives `eidetic remember`: accepts one record as a JSON object, or a batch as NDJSON on stdin for bulk ingest. Accepts `supersedes` (id of the record this one replaces, for within-scope shadowing via `sweep`) and `links` (list of related-memory ids).
